# pathos.cloud Dashboard UI Reference

> Canonical reference for the dashboard layer. For *how* the dashboard mirrors the API + which architectural role it plays, see [docs/architecture/dashboard.md](../../docs/architecture/dashboard.md). For design rationale, see [docs/concepts/](../../docs/concepts/).

Quasar v2 (Vue 3) | Javascript | SCSS | Webpack | Axios

## Setup (after `npm init quasar`)

When running `npm init quasar` from project root choose:
- App with Quasar CLI
- Project folder: `dashboard`
- Quasar version: Quasar v2 (Vue 3)
- Script type: **Javascript**
- Quasar App CLI variant: **Webpack**
- CSS preprocessor: **Sass with SCSS syntax**
- Features: **Axios, Vue-Router, Pinia, ESLint**
- ESLint preset: Standard

Then replace/add the pre-built files in `src/`.

## Service mirror (src/services/)

Each frontend service is a thin axios wrapper around one backend resource. Conventions:
- Return the raw API payload (`{ success, ...payload }` or `{ success: false, error }`).
- Components handle both shapes inline.
- The axios instance lives in `services/api.js` with a request-interceptor that injects the JWT and a 401 interceptor that clears auth + redirects to `/auth`.

| File | Backs |
|------|-------|
| `api.js` | the axios instance |
| `auth.service.js` | `/api/identity/*` |
| `node.service.js` | `/api/nodes/*` |
| `label.service.js` | `/api/labels/*` |
| `post.service.js` | `/api/posts/*` — **canonical post API client** |
| `nodeInteraction.service.js` | legacy `/api/nodes/:id/{vote,interactions,...}` |
| `skeleton.service.js` | `/api/skeletons/*` — includes `items(id)` for the spine-pairs walk |
| `ref.service.js` | `/api/refs/*` — `summary`, `search`, and `surround(ref, limit)` (the one call behind `ElementSurround`) |
| `verify.service.js` | The truth spine's read surface (Thread B, 2026-07-30): `verify(ref)` → `GET /api/verify/<kind>/<hash>` report for `ProvenanceBadge`, `downloadBundle(ref)` → the export bundle handed to the browser as a JSON download (offline-verifiable via the api repo's `scripts/verify-bundle.mjs`) |
| `entity.service.js` additions (2026-07-30) | `createAlterEgo` grew `inherit {photo, bio}` + `discloseOrigin`; `origin(id)` → the lineage chain (disclosure-gated); `setOriginDisclosure(id, bool)` — the owner's switch |
| `feed.service.js` | `/api/feed/*` |
| `components/shared/GlobalSearch.vue` | **Thread E's face** (2026-07-30): the NavigationBar search button + top-anchored dialog over `GET /api/search` — content hits (snippet rows → node viewer) + by-name chips (entities/labels/posts → their viewers). Debounced 350ms; empty state names the access doctrine so "nothing" reads as privacy working |
| `components/entities/OriginTree.vue` | **The ORIGIN CONSTELLATION** (2026-07-30): an entity's lineage as a night sky — three drifting CSS starfield layers over deep ink, the chain rendered BOTTOM-UP (you at the bottom, the thread unraveling through alter-egos to the root, then the invite chain climbing to the PIONEER's gold-gradient star). The thread is one repeated SVG bezier whose stroke DRAWS upward (dashoffset animation) staggered per node — the unravel IS the animation; edges captioned "unravels to" (teal) / "invited by" (gold). A concealed edge renders as FOG ("the thread fades here") — the server already refused, the sky just says so. Owners of an alter-ego get the amber disclosure toggle ON the sky. Opened by the "Origin constellation" button on `EntityProfilePage`'s side column |
| `components/entities/AlterEgoPanel.vue` (2026-07-30 window) | The inline creation form became a DIALOG (`.ego-create`): name + bio + **inherit from root** (photo / bio checkboxes) + the **origin disclosure toggle** (amber; default private — the label states the consequence in plain words, both ways). The choice lives as the profile's ORIGIN record; flippable later from the constellation |
| `nav.service.js` | `/api/nav/*` |
| `pin.service.js` | `/api/pins/*` |
| `access.service.js` | `/api/access/*` — `status`, `grant`, `publish`, `tree` (the pre-publish reference tree), `request` (locked chip → owner poll; ships with chats). NOTE: `api.js` is a **default** export — `import api from './api'`, not `{ api }` |
| `chat.service.js` | `/api/chats/*` — `list`, `open(memberIds)` (find-or-create), `feed(chatId)`, `send(chatId, content, shareRefs)` |
| `poll.service.js` | `/api/polls/*` — `vote(pollId, 'yes'\|'no')` (decider-only, final) |
| `claim.service.js` | `/api/claims/*` (Thread D reader surface, 2026-07-31) — `get`, `list`, `vote(id, {stance, ref, note})` (dispute ref mandatory, `<kind>/<hash>` form), `retract`, `create` |
| `embed.service.js` | `/api/embeds/*` — `resolve(url)` (a web link → the `EmbedFrame` descriptor, `embed: null` when no rule matches) and `rules()`. Only for URLs that are not yet nodes: a node read already carries `node.embed` |

## Stores (src/stores/)

| Store | What it holds |
|-------|---------------|
| `auth.js` | `token`, `user`, `systemStatus`. Boot calls `/api/identity/verify`; on 401 the store is cleared and the router redirects to `/auth`. |
| `navigation.js` | Persistent navigation history + checkpoints + the **StateHolder map** (`states`, keyed by route: per-element viewer state persisted via `/api/nav/state`) + back/forward **return-marking** (`lastReturn`, consumed by viewers to halo the element you came back from). Driven by the bottom `NavigationBar`. See [docs/concepts/navigation.md](../../docs/concepts/navigation.md). |
| `maker.js` / `uploader.js` | The two docks' content state: draft/upload tabs (localStorage-persisted) + own window flags (`isOpen`/`isMinimized`/`isMaximized`). Each exports the shared minitab label helper (`draftLabel` / `uploadLabel`). |
| `labelMaker.js` | The label maker dock's window flags + the selected label (session-only — label mutations are single round-trips, nothing to draft). `openAt(label)` opens focused on a given label. |
| `chat.js` | The chat dock's window flags (maker-pattern) + `activeChatId` + per-conversation composer drafts — localStorage `pathos_chat`. |
| `mediaViewers.js` | **The floating media viewer family** (2026-08-04, `docs/plans/floating-media-viewer.md`): N simultaneous viewer records `{ id, node, rect, natural, minimized, maximized }` + `spawn(node)` (one viewer per node — respawn restores/fronts) / `close` / `minimize` / `restore` / `toggleMaximize` / `setRect` / `setNatural`. Per-viewer flags live here (the family-store precedent); z comes from `windows.focus('media:<id>')` — the windows store's z half is key-agnostic. Nothing persisted: a preview window is session furniture. |
| `dashboard.js` | The **dashboard dock's** window flags (2026-08-10), the `labelMaker` pattern: `isOpen`/`isMinimized`/`isMaximized` and nothing else — the panel is empty by design, so there is nothing to draft and nothing to persist. z-order under the `dashboard` key in windows.js, which is what puts it in the same stack as the other five creation docks. `close()` deliberately KEEPS `isMaximized`, the way every dock does: a window you left maximized should come back that way. |
| `flyouts.js` | **The flyout family's window flags** (2026-08-10) — today just the skeleton flyout's `minimized`/`maximized` plus the `label`/`icon` its parked tab is drawn from. Its CONTENT stays on `FeedPage` (`selected`, `flyoutRef` are the feed's own state); only the window state lives here, because it is the one part TWO surfaces must see — the page draws the box and `MediaTabsBar`, on the far side of the layout, draws the tab it parks into. Not persisted: a parked flyout is session furniture, and `resetSkeleton()` on FeedPage's unmount is what stops a tab from outliving the box it restores. |
| `geo.js` | Location-sharing consent (2026-07-19). `maybeAsk()` (MainLayout onMounted) shows the one-time "Anchor your moments?" dialog; agreeing triggers the browser geolocation prompt and `capture()` stores the coords **city-rounded to 1 decimal** as localStorage `pathos_geo` (`'lat,lon'`; choice remembered in `pathos_geo_choice`). `services/api.js` attaches it to every request as `X-Pathos-Geo`, so the API stamps every moment the user mints. Precise coordinates never leave the browser; the driver pre-declines so scripted flows never see the dialog. |
| `windows.js` | **Window manager** for the bottom-docked windows (`maker`, `uploader`, `skeletonBuilder`, `labelMaker`, `chat`, `dashboard` since 2026-08-10, `stack`, `pins`): z-order stack (`focus`/`release`, `zOf(key)` → 2400+) so a newly opened window covers the previous one and closing/minimizing it reveals that one again. Also owns the stack/pins window flags (`panels.*`, localStorage-persisted, + `openPanel`/`closePanel`/`minimizePanel`/`restorePanel`/`togglePanel`), the **side-column geometry**: `railKeys` (open stack/pins), `railWidth` (the one-column width, kept as a **permanent reserve** — page content pads right by it and nothing reflows when a panel expands; each side bar is ONE element whose parked presentation is the SAME panel narrowed to a thin icon column at the same screen edge, `right: 0` — `is-parked`, 2026-07-24 third pass, the separate rail tree + `railOffsetOf` are gone) and `dockRight` (every OTHER expanded window shifts left by this so it renders *beside* the parked column, via the `--dock-right` CSS var on `.dock-window`), plus `footerPanelInset` (how far the minitab strip shifts left to clear the pins widget — `RAIL_W` when parked, `PANEL_W` when the panel is expanded at the screen edge), and the transient **`split`** state (skeleton builder left + companion maker/uploader right, 50vw each via `.is-split-left`/`.is-split-right`) — **dormant** since the July 2026 schema/instance split (nothing calls `enterSplit` now that instance population moved to the SkeletonPage viewer; the docks just float over the page). |

## Composables (src/composables/)

| Composable | Role |
|------------|------|
| `useStateHolder.js` | ⭐ The per-viewer StateHolder seam: persisted reactive `state` (debounced + flushed on leave), approximate scroll restore (window and/or tracked containers, proportional + height-settled), the luminous-yellow return halo on `[data-nav-focus]` elements, `describe()` (feeds real titles/hashes to the stack panel), `recordInteraction()` (extends the element's pathchain interaction path). Wired into: LabelExplorerPage (dug-open square set), PostViewerPage, NodeDetailPage, LabelDetailPage, SkeletonPage, FileTreePage (re-reveals last address), `posts/FeedStream.vue` (the feed's holder moved out of the page into the stream COMPONENT at the end of 2026-07-25 — `trackScroll: false` + `trackContainer(wellEl, 'feed')`, since on `/feed` the scroller is the container's well and the page never scrolls; `onBeforeRouteLeave` still fires from a child of the routed component — and since 2026-08-06 `state.headY`, where the draggable head box was left standing: an ARRANGEMENT of the surface, not a lens you look through, so coming back from a post to find the head snapped back to the top would read as the drag having been undone. Written on pointer RELEASE, one row per gesture), FeedPageLegacy. |
| `useElementSurround.js` | The surround seam (2026-07-14): one `refService.surround(ref, limit)` call resolving an element's skeleton walk + surround sections (author / createdAt / forkedFrom / labels / versions / forks / comments / scores) for `shared/ElementSurround.vue`. Used by LinkViewerPage, PathViewerPage, LabelDetailPage sidebars. |
| `useMediaArena.js` | **The media viewers' two regions** (2026-08-04; split **2026-08-05** on a user ask) — one module-singleton measurement set (viewport + the real `.frieze-header`/`.nav-footer` boxes, one resize listener) behind two computeds. `arena {x,y,w,h}` = where a window is **born**: desktop = the RIGHT HALF (below the crown, above the nav footer, left of the stack/pins rail, 14px inset); `windows.isMobile` portrait = the TOP HALF (over the content by design, 8px inset); landscape phone = the whole viewport between the bands — caught by a **height ≤ 500px gate**, because the store's width-only 600px gate reads a rotated phone as desktop. `roam` = where it may **go**: the WHOLE viewport, no inset and no band subtraction. The arena's ceiling is the crown strip's measured `getBoundingClientRect().bottom`, NOT its height (2026-08-05): with the media tabs band parked above it the strip no longer starts at 0, while `friezeH` — which feeds `chromeOf`, i.e. the band drawn INSIDE a viewer — still has to be the height. Spawn fit asks the arena; drag clamps, resize maxima and the stale-rect re-clamp ask the roam. They were ONE region until 08-05, which made the right half a fence — the window could not be pulled over the page it came from, and every rail/orientation change hauled it back. The bands are not carved out of the roam because neither hides what matters: the crown strip paints UNDER a viewer (3000 vs 3010+) and the nav footer can only cover its bottom, so the header stays visible and grabbable — and `clampRect` keeps the whole rect on screen. |
| `useMediaWindowGestures.js` | The media window's drag + resize (2026-08-04) — one Pointer-Events listener set (mouse + touch): bar `pointerdown` (non-button, `closest('button')` guard) = **move**, eight rim handles = **aspect-locked resize** (media ratio from `viewer.natural`; anchor = opposite edge/corner; floor = `minSize()` — the header shrink limit; ceilings = anchor-relative **roam** distances). Every move commits through `store.setRect` → `clampRect` (drag past the boundary pins at the farthest legal spot). The composable **never sees the arena** since 2026-08-05: both gestures clamp to the whole viewport, and they must use the SAME region — an arena-clamped resize of a window parked outside the arena computes a negative ceiling and `clampRect` then teleports it back into the right half. BottomSplitter's hygiene: body userSelect/cursor parked during, listeners torn down on release AND unmount, `is-grabbed`/`is-resizing` kill transitions and light the edges (`--teal-13` ring). Gestures disabled while maximized; a stale rect re-clamps at gesture start. |

## Layouts

| Layout | Used by | What it provides |
|--------|---------|------------------|
| `AuthLayout` | `/auth` | Centered, glass-style auth surface, crowned by the fixed `FriezeHeader` (page container pads down by `--frieze-h`) |
| `MainLayout` | every authenticated page | Fixed `FriezeHeader` crown strip over everything + page container (pads right by `windows.railWidth` so the parked side columns never cover content, and down by `--frieze-h` so content clears the frieze; the **drawer does NOT** — since 2026-07-24 it runs from the screen's top-left corner OVER the crown strip, and **since 2026-08-02 it runs to the WINDOW FLOOR too** (user ask): `bottom: 0` at **`z 3120`**, so the column owns the full window height and LIES OVER the nav bar (3110) instead of stopping at its top edge. That bar was raised above everything on the same day precisely so nothing could paint on it; the drawer is the ONE deliberate exception and it covers no control — the bar's left cluster is empty while the drawer stands (see `.drawer-footer` below) and even expanded the drawer is 220px, far short of the right cluster. It covers the bar's left end as well (and covered the LEFT end of the frieze footer band until that band's 2026-08-04 retirement). Its last `--nav-bar-h` is therefore the bar's own row, and **`.drawer-footer` rebuilds that row inside the drawer** (with a `.drawer-floor-frieze` **FriezeBar under it**, un-flipped like the drawer's own section dividers, continuing the floor band across this column — in the crown strip's pale palette since 2026-08-04; see NavigationBar): brown-1 plaque, a brown-3 `border-top` landing on the same pixel as the bar's, and the 41px brown-4 `.drawer-burger-slot` + 1px hairline at its left end holding the 24px inverted-brown chip (28px before the bar shortened) — NavigationBar's `.burger-slot` to the pixel (verified: the chip's box is x 7…35 whichever surface draws it). **THE BURGER LIVES HERE NOW**, and its only job is to CLOSE (`drawer = false`): while the drawer stands there is nothing to open, and when it goes the bar grows its own burger back in exactly that spot. Mini state: the content box is 41px, so the slot's own hairline falls outside it and the drawer's brown-3 `border-right` stands in for it — same ink, same pixel (`overflow: hidden` on the block does the clipping). Quasar's modal `.q-drawer__backdrop` was bumped 2999 → **3115** the same day: under the drawer (3120) but OVER the bar, so on a phone the bar dims with the page instead of poking out of a modal, lit and still clickable. **Collapsed width = `--dock-rail-w` (42px) since 2026-07-31** — it mirrors the stack/pins parked column so the two screen edges wear one rail width each, and the mini rail runs unbroken from the crown strip to the bottom of the screen (its own footer block continuing it now, the nav bar's block only when the drawer is gone). The hard-coded `:mini-width="42"` became **`:mini-width="drawerRailW"`** that day — the token read once in `setup()` (`getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--dock-rail-w')`, fallback 42), because three surfaces now depend on that one number and a drift shows as a step at the bottom-left corner. Note the local `drawerRailW` is NOT `windows.railWidth` (the page container's right reserve for the side widgets); the list is densified to make the 32px chip work: item margins `2px 5px`, `min-height: 40px`, frieze dividers `6px 0`, section headers `6px 14px 3px`. **The IDENTITY section is `profile → Organizations → Log out`, and the profile row is a DENSE IDENTITY BLOCK since 2026-08-02** (`.drawer-profile-item`), not the word "Profile": the feed card's author section — `EntityAvatar` at 28px, display name over `@handle`, the same 1.15 leading that makes two lines read as one stamp, the same `display_name || username || entity #id` fall-back chain — re-coated for the drawer in `--brown-8` ink on the brown-1 plaque (`.drawer-profile-name` 0.92em/700, `.drawer-profile-handle` 0.78em mono at brown-8/.62). It is where the acting identity lives now that the nav bar carries none. `EntityAvatar` gets the auth store's `user` object, which has no `photo` key, so it resolves the face itself through the session-cached `entitySummary` — one request, shared with every other chip for that entity. The **MASK CHIP** (`.drawer-profile-mask` — inverted-brown `theater_comedy` in a 15px circle with a brown-1 ring) floats on the AVATAR's bottom-right corner rather than beside the name, because Quasar's mini drawer keeps only the `avatar` section: acting as an alter-ego has to stay visible in the 42px rail state, which is the state the drawer is in most of the time. `Log out` (`.drawer-logout-item`) came off the nav bar with it and CLOSES the section below the destinations, drawn muted — one loud affordance per drawer, and that one is Back) + bottom `NavigationBar` (the `FriezeFooter` band that stood on its top edge from 2026-07-25 was retired 2026-08-04 — the floor frieze band inside the footer wears its pale palette instead) + the six always-mounted docked windows (`MakerDock`, `UploaderDock`, `SkeletonBuilderDock`, `LabelMakerDock`, `StackPanel`, `PinsDrawer`), z-ordered by `stores/windows.js` + `media/MediaViewerHost.vue` (2026-08-04 — the floating media viewer family and its minimize sliver; see the media rows below). Routes the pin refresh key both ways (tack ↔ drawer), and routes maker/uploader `created` events into an active SkeletonPage populate session's fresh tray (`skeletonBuilder.deliverFresh`) before falling back to post/node navigation. **Mobile pass (Thread H item 1, 2026-07-31, one `600px` breakpoint kept in sync with `MOBILE_MQ` in `stores/windows.js`)**: the drawer model **starts closed under 1024px** — below `show-if-above`'s breakpoint the q-drawer is an OVERLAY, and a `true` model at boot drops its fullscreen `.q-drawer__backdrop` over the page, killing every tap (the menu button toggles it as an overlay instead); `windows.initViewportWatch()` (called here on mount) keeps `windows.isMobile` live, which zeroes `railWidth`/`footerPanelInset` while the CSS hides the stack/pins widgets — so under 600px the layout is **one surface at a time**: full-width feed container (95% of the track) with the flyout overlaying it (2.5% margins), full-SCREEN creation docks (`left/right: 0`, `top: var(--frieze-h)` in `_components.scss` — change request #675), full-width chat dock (its scoped block — a global override loses to scoped specificity) with the conversations column narrowed to `minmax(110px, 34%)`, and the instance editor's field/Populate grid stacked.  **`<router-view @pins-changed="pinsRefreshKey++">`** (2026-08-07) — a page can pin too now (the feed card's cap), and the pins widget that has to reload is a sibling of the page container, not of the page. Vue Router forwards attrs, listeners included, to the matched component, so any page that DECLARES the emit is heard here and the rest are unaffected — same destination as `MediaViewerHost`'s tack and the nav bar's. The declaration is load-bearing: an undeclared emit falls through to the page's root element as a DOM listener, where a custom event name never fires (see specs/gotchas.md). **THE DASHBOARD PANEL IS A DOCKED WINDOW HERE (2026-08-10)** — `<DashboardDock />` beside the other seven, and nothing else. It spent one pass as a floating `.dashboard-flyout` slot owned by this file (fixed, `left: 52.5%`, z 3060, two plain refs and an Escape handler of its own); the second ask that day moved it onto the bar with the creative windows, so its flags live in `stores/dashboard.js` like every other dock's — which is what lets the nav bar draw both its button and its minitab without this layout passing anything down. The slot, the refs, the Escape listener, the `dashboard-open` prop and this file's only `@media (max-width: 600px)` block went with it. |

## Pages

| Route | Page | What it does |
|-------|------|-------------|
| `/auth` | `AuthPage` | One frieze-crowned brown plaque (2026-07-29 redesign): metallic wordmark + two-temperature slogan ("mind at rest" mint / "heart ablaze" ember), enter\|join halves side by side (no tabs; hairline divide goes horizontal on wrap), "I've got a secret" invite well w/ mint aurora, mint/ember CTAs, brown-2 field wells. The *enter* half toggles into a **recover** form (Thread H, 2026-07-31 — the `.auth-toggle` dotted-underline text control under the CTA): recovery secret in the same aurora well (`restart_alt` icon, "A vouch from your inviter") + username + new password (min 8) → `authStore.recover` (`POST /identity/recovery/reset`), a success routing straight to `/feed` logged in; head retitles enter→recover, epigraph hides while recovering |
| `/landing` | *(static, not a Vue route)* | The pre-2026-07-29 production landing preserved verbatim under `public/landing/` — history-mode router base + Vite assets base both patched to `/landing/` in the minified bundle, `tomb.png` path fixed; allegue's whim, served beside the SPA. **Its favicon set is the SPA's favicon too since 2026-08-03**: the pathos mark (turbine rosette in a cyan ring) copied byte-identical from `landing/` up to `public/{favicon.ico, icons/favicon-16·32·96·128.png}` and declared in `src/index.template.html` (the template linked `icons/favicon-16/32` for months while `public/icons/` didn't exist — tabs were blank). The landing copy stays byte-frozen; the root set is the app's own |
| `/feed` | `FeedPage` | **New container layout (2026-07-25)** — the page is the geometry, `components/posts/FeedStream.vue` is the content in it (the shell stood EMPTY for the first half of 2026-07-25, with the stream parked in `FeedPageLegacy.vue`; the stream moved INTO the container at the end of that day — see the FeedStream row): ONE transparent horizontal scroll track (`.feed-track`, `overflow-x: auto`) sized to the free window slot — `height: calc(100vh - var(--nav-footer-h))` on the `q-page` (left/right come free: MainLayout's `q-page-container` already pads left by the drawer and right by `windows.railWidth`, the parked stack/pins column) — holding one **LEFT-PACKED Feed Container** at `flex: 0 0 45%` of the track (2026-08-02 user ask, +5 over 2026-07-27's 40% — that day walked 50% → 30% → 40%; 50% stood from the end of 2026-07-25), full slot height, **`--grey-2` coat** (2026-08-07, user ask, via one setting on `--grey-5`) — **which must be set together with `FeedStream`'s scroll bed**, since the bed covers this box edge to edge (measured, both `95,5,571,900`; the only container pixels outside it sit behind the two opaque frieze bars), so a container-only change is true in the stylesheet and invisible on screen — a trap that has cost two passes, see [gotchas](../../specs/gotchas.md). ⚠ At this tone the field is LIGHTER than the post cards (`rgb(245,245,245)` against their veiled `rgb(242,239,234)`), inverting the figure/ground held since 2026-08-06: the box is a PAGE with slightly darker sheets on it rather than a plate with pale sheets lying on it, and the card's tone stack was tuned for the other reading. Not following it: the frieze bars (an `--indigo-8` frame since that morning), the HEAD BOX and the card's label-rail tray, all still `--grey-4` — the head box's "separated by DEPTH, not tone" rule and the tray's "the bed tone" rule are stated-but-false until someone decides. It was **`--grey-4`** before, and that WAS the same plaque tone its frieze edges wear — `--indigo-1` until 2026-08-05, when the bars went neutral in the morning's ask and the box followed them in the evening's, the rule "box and edges are one material" holding throughout while the tone under it moved twice; **that rule is RETIRED as of 2026-08-07**, when the bars inverted to an `--indigo-8` plaque and the box did NOT follow — the coat stays here and the bars are a dark FRAME standing on it, the relation the head box's inner posts have had since 08-06; FeedStream's head band and scroll bed came along, so the whole field was one plaque and the `--indigo-1` post cards read as objects LYING ON it — **2026-08-06 then walked the bed to `--grey-5` and `--grey-3` and back here**, settling the step onto the CARD instead (`--grey-3`, one above the plaque): the box — bars, container, head band, bed — is one material with no edge inside it, and the card is the pale thing lying on it. **The HEAD left that list later the same day**, when it stopped being a band: `FeedHeadBox` is `--grey-3` on the plate, the card's own step, because it is a thing lying on the box rather than the box showing through — bars, container and bed are unchanged), with a **`5%` `margin-left`** of daylight off the drawer's edge (percentages of the TRACK, i.e. of the screen left over by the drawer and the rail — 669px + 67px on a 1338px track). The box **grew from 40% by five points off EACH side** at the end of 2026-07-25, and the **left five came out of the drawer gap** (`10%` → `5%`, itself a hairline `8px` before that morning), so that edge advanced toward the drawer while the right edge simply reached five further into the track; gap + box went 10 + 40 = 50% → 5 + 50 = **55%** of the track (2.5 + 40 = **42.5%** from 2026-07-27, the drawer gap having been halved in the same ask; 2.5 + 45 = **47.5%** since 2026-08-02). That widening is also what forced **`min-width: 0` on `.feed-container`** (end of 2026-07-25, the day the box stopped being empty): a flex item's default `min-width: auto` floors it at its content's **min-content** size, and that floor OUTRANKS a percentage flex-basis — the stream's widest nowrap post title measured the box out to **54.61%** (731px) instead of 50% until the floor was zeroed here *and* down FeedStream's own chain (pane → well → `.post-square__name`, where the title's existing ellipsis then does the work). Verify a geometry change by ratio, never by eye: `boxW / trackW` should read exactly `50`. **The slot starts at the window's TOP EDGE and the container OVERLAPS the crown strip** (end of 2026-07-25 — it began below it): the page carries `margin-top: calc(-1 * var(--frieze-h))`, which cancels the `padding-top: var(--frieze-h)` `q-page-container` stamps for the strip, so the track's box runs up to `y = 0` (verified: container `top: 0`, `height: 852` at 900px tall = viewport − the nav bar as it then was (48px; the bar's row is `--nav-bar-h` 32px since 2026-08-02, with `--nav-footer-h` one `--frieze-h` more, and on desktop this column runs the full 900 anyway — measured `top 0 → bottom 900`), and `documentElement.scrollHeight` still 900). Reaching the strip's band is only half of it — `FriezeHeader` is `position: fixed; z-index: 3000`, so the OPAQUE container must outrank it to actually cover it, and it does with `position: relative; z-index: 3001`. That lift deliberately rides the **container**, not the track: the track is transparent and fills the whole slot, so lifting it would hang an invisible 3001 sheet over every dock (2400+) in the page's area for no paint benefit. The left drawer (3050) still outranks the container, exactly as it outranks the strip. The container also casts a **slight lateral shadow** off each frieze edge — `box-shadow: ∓8px 0 18px -4px rgba(0,0,0,.55)`, twice — so it reads as standing above the page: the negative spread keeps it tight to the edges and the track's `overflow-y: hidden` trims any top/bottom bleed, leaving the effect strictly sideways. The alpha is high *for a slight shadow* by necessity — it falls on the page's near-black `body--dark` canvas (`rgb(38,38,38)`), where the first pass at `.30` only darkened the edge to 33 and was invisible; `.55` lands it at 26 and ramps back to canvas over ~15px, which reads. It was centred at 30% until 2026-07-25. Two notes on that pair of changes: `justify-content: flex-start` is also the **correct anchoring for a horizontal scroller** — under `center`, a content run wider than the track is clipped at the START and unreachable, since `scrollLeft` cannot go negative; and the gap is carried as the ITEM's `margin-left` rather than the track's `padding-left`, because a percentage flex-basis resolves against the flex container's CONTENT box — padding would quietly make the box a percentage of (track − gap) instead of a true track share (verified across widths: 535.2px = 40.00% of a 1338px track, unchanged when the gap went 8px → 10%; 669px = 50.00% with the gap at 5%; measured 40.00% with the 2.50% gap after 2026-07-27's resizes; as a margin both percentages share that one basis and simply add, whereas as padding a 5% gap would shrink the box below its stated share). That container is itself a flex row whose two side EDGES are vertical frieze bars (2026-07-25, indigo colorway — lips facing inward, with `.feed-container__body` flexing between them); the bars REPLACED its plain side borders, each carrying that hairline as its own lip (lime-3 at first, `--indigo-3` once the bar's colorway settled, then one afternoon at `--grey-6`, one evening at `--indigo-6` and `--indigo-4` mid-2026-08-06 — the lip having been a WAVE tone throughout, with only the wave it named moving: one → two → back to one — and **since late 2026-08-06 the PLAQUE's own tone instead, i.e. no line at all**: `--grey-4` then, `--indigo-8` since the bars inverted on 08-07, the lip travelling with the plaque by rule; see the FriezeBarVertical row), and they live INSIDE the percentage so it covers the whole box, edges included — `.feed-container__body` is the field between them and CLIPS (`overflow: hidden`), because FeedStream carries its own scrolling well and nothing in it may spill past the frieze edges or grow the page. They are a **MIRRORED PAIR** (end of 2026-07-25 — the same bar twice before that): `<FriezeBarVertical lip="right" />` on the LEFT (**variant A**), `<FriezeBarVerticalB lip="left" />` on the RIGHT (**variant B** — A with its wave pattern flipped horizontally), so the two edges reflect each other across the box instead of repeating. The container **hovers over the `FriezeFooter` band too** (end of 2026-07-25): its `height: calc(100vh - var(--nav-footer-h))` puts its bottom edge on the nav bar's top edge, exactly the band's box, and its z 3001 outranks the band's 3000 — so on `/feed` the container overlays the page's bottom band the same way it runs up over the crown strip at the top. Nothing was changed to get that; the geometry already lined up. **ON DESKTOP THE COLUMN NOW RUNS PAST THE BAR TO THE WINDOW'S FLOOR (2026-08-02, user ask: "extend the public feed scroller all the way to the bottom, hovering on top of the bottom nav bar, but only on desktop mode")** — inside `@media (min-width: 1024px)` the page takes `height: 100vh` plus a matching **negative `margin-bottom: calc(-1 * var(--nav-footer-h))`**, so the box paints a nav bar lower while contributing nothing extra to flow (`q-page-container` pads its bottom by the fixed footer's height; without the negative margin the page is one nav-bar height taller than the slot and the window grows a vertical scrollbar — the very thing the empty `:style-fn` below exists to prevent). The stream scrolls THROUGH that last strip, so the column reads as one uninterrupted run from the crown strip to the floor instead of a panel on a shelf. Half of the change lives in `NavigationBar.vue`: the bar is the topmost fixed chrome at z 3110, so the container's 3001 could not paint over it, and the container deliberately does NOT climb — anything over 3110 is over EVERYTHING, and every right-half dock (and any maximized one, 3010+) would be sliced by this column on this very page. The **bar** steps down to **2999 on `/feed` only** (`.nav-footer--underlaid`, same breakpoint), the one change that leaves docks, drawer, side widgets, minitab strip and both frieze bands exactly as they were; its cost is that on this route a dock's or the drawer's drop shadow can wash the plaque again. **Mobile keeps the shelf** (below 1024px the page still stops at the bar's top edge and the bar stays 3110): the bar is the only chrome down there, and covering it would put post cards under the thumb that opens the menu. The **flyout holds still** — its `bottom` restates `var(--nav-footer-h)` on top of the band inset at that breakpoint, since the page's box it is positioned against just grew. Measured: at 1400×900 page + container bottom = 900 with `documentElement.scrollHeight` 900 and a hit-test 6px off the floor inside the container landing on a post card, not the bar; at 1000×800 both stop at 752 and that same point hits `.nav-center`; flyout bottom = 900 − `--nav-footer-h` − `--frieze-h` − 14 (811 back when those were 48 and 27; both tokens shrank on 2026-08-02, so the arithmetic holds and the number moved), unchanged. The page passes an empty `:style-fn` so Quasar's inline layout-derived `min-height` never makes the page taller than the slot (that would hand the window a vertical scrollbar). **It also owns the POST INFORMATION FLYOUT** (2026-07-26): `selected` (the WHOLE feed item — `GET /feed` already answered with everything the panel shows, so opening the box costs no request), an `onSelect` that TOGGLES, `clearSelection`, and an Escape `keydown` listener; `FeedStream` gets `:selected-id` down and emits `select` up. **Since 2026-08-07 the same box has a second door**: the card cap's `open_in_new` emits `open-skeleton`, and `onOpenSkeleton` toggles `flyoutRef` to the post's bare id — the `skeletonRef` path the `?flyout=` query param opened in 2026-07-31, which mounts `SkeletonMini` instead of `FeedPostPanel`. So one box shows either face of a post (its post panel, or its slots and spine), the two are mutually exclusive by construction (each handler nulls the other's state), and `:flyout-id` goes back down only so the cap's button can light. The box itself is `skeletons/SkeletonFlyout.vue`, mounted in **`.feed-flyout`** — a sibling of the TRACK, not of the stream, because `.feed-container__body` clips its content and nothing placed inside it could ever reach the free half of the window. Positioned against the PAGE's box, which IS the free window slot (the layout already carved the drawer off its left and `railWidth` off its right), so "the right half of the available screen" needs no measurement: `top`/`bottom: calc(var(--frieze-h) + var(--flyout-gap))` float it strictly BETWEEN the crown strip and the frieze footer **and clear of both** — the token is the band's height, the **`--flyout-gap: 14px`** is the daylight that makes the box hover rather than wedge between them (2026-07-26, second pass; it was flush against each band, so a carved wave ran straight into the plaque's rounded corner at either end). Flat px and not the horizontal `5%`: that percentage resolves against the slot's WIDTH (~67px here), which a 770px-tall box in a 1342px-wide slot cannot spare, and a flat value keeps both ends equal at every window height. `left: 52.5%` / `right: 5%` (restated with each 2026-07-27 container resize) put it on the container's own rhythm — **2.5% gap │ container 45% │ 5% gap │ flyout 42.5% │ 5% gap** (2026-08-02), the left edge being the container's 47.5% plus the flyout's own 5% gap (the two gaps around the flyout deliberately did not follow the halved drawer gap). Measured (2026-08-02, container 45%): flyout left 52.5%, right 5%. `z-index: 3002` clears the frieze bands (3000) and the container (3001) and stays under the drawer (3050) and the pinned column (3100) — which is why `.feed-page` gained `position: relative` and deliberately **no** z-index ([gotchas](../../specs/gotchas.md): a z-index there makes the page one stacking context and the flyout sinks under both bands). Enters/leaves with a 0.16s fade + 14px slide from the right. The track-scroll limit is CLOSED (2026-07-31, Thread H): the page mirrors the track's `scrollLeft` (passive scroll listener) into an inline `left`/`right` override on `.feed-flyout` — left/right and not transform, since the enter/leave transition owns transform — so when the track overflows and scrolls, the box rides with the content instead of hanging over whatever slides under it  **A GRADED SIDE FRAME, built outside-in on 2026-08-07** (three user asks, side edges only — nothing on the ends): `.feed-container` takes a 1px **`--indigo-4`** rim, the two frieze bars are the **`--indigo-9`** plates between them (their own 1px edges are plaque-toned and draw no line — they reserve a margin), and `.feed-stream__well` closes it with a 1px **`--indigo-6`** line, which is the edge the post cards actually run against. Three marks, three jobs, three levels of one family, and they never touch — `-6` is Material 500, the pure hue, read against a `--grey-2` bed and cream cards, where `-4` is read against the page's near-black canvas and can afford to be lighter; the plates standing between them are what make the set read as three marks rather than a gradient. The container's border lands on the box's OUTER silhouette (it is the flex PARENT of the bars) and therefore sits inside the lateral `box-shadow` rather than being softened by it. `border-box` throughout so nothing grows: the container holds an exact 45% of the track (`documentElement.scrollWidth` = `innerWidth`, verified) and the well's 2px narrows `.feed-stream`, which the ResizeObserver picks up so `--post-square-max` follows on its own (558px measured, card 558px). Also that day: **the head box's two inner posts went `0.6 × --frieze-h`** — `--fhead-post-w`, ONE number driving both their `--frieze-bar-v-slim-w` and the content's inset past them — up from the slim variant's `/ 2`, which the same day's side-border pass had taken under the motif floor. `0.6` puts a mask row at ~0.85px, the same reading the container's own `0.8` bars get.  **The FIELD is back at `--grey-4`** (2026-08-07, after one setting at `--grey-2`): that tone had the field lighter than the veiled post cards (rgb 245,245,245 vs 242,239,234) — three levels, INVERTED, the box reading as a page with darker sheets on it — and `--grey-4` restores the plate-with-pale-sheets reading the card's whole tone stack was built for, at thirteen levels. ⚠ **`.feed-container` and `.feed-stream__well` must carry the same coat**: the bed covers the container edge to edge (both measured `95,5,571,900`), so setting one alone is either invisible or a seam — a trap that has now cost three passes. The feed head box's `--fhead-face` is also `--grey-4`, i.e. the box and its field are one tone; that is by its own design, which separates it by DEPTH (its indigo-7 / indigo-5 edges and its cast) rather than by tone. |
| — | `FeedPageLegacy.vue` | Unrouted. The previous `/feed` as a FULL-PAGE surface: post-square stream (select-a-card) beside a `FeedPostPanel` detail column, `useStateHolder`-backed. Kept **verbatim** so the old surface can be restored by pointing the `/feed` route back at it (note it is no longer `FeedPostPanel`'s only live consumer — `skeletons/SkeletonFlyout.vue` mounts the same panel since 2026-07-26) — which is why `components/posts/FeedStream.vue` is a refit rather than an extraction: the card styles are duplicated on purpose, and this file is the frozen restore point, not a live dependency. Edit FeedStream for anything you actually want on `/feed` |
| `/mercury-playground` | `MercuryPlaygroundPage` | Design sandbox (left drawer hidden via `meta: { hideDrawer: true }`): gradient builder over the ServiceConsole palette (radial/linear, position/angle sliders, editable stops, click-palette-to-add-stop, copyable CSS), live previews on switchable canvas colors — boxes in the repo's `rounded-*` utilities plus a q-btn variant row (unelevated/rounded/push/glossy/round/lg) — and 13 clickable presets lifted from the ServiceConsole stylesheet that load into the builder. Also a **pixel grid lab**: rows × cols of 3-layer "pixels" (offset shadow / main / translucent glass w/ backdrop blur), one global panel drives color+alpha+radius per layer for all pixels, and a mini control grid below toggles single pixels on/off or opens a per-pixel override editor (per-layer colors, keyed `row-col`); built to later take one background painted across the whole grid. A **wave frieze** toggle constrains the grid to a 22-row × 20-col-period stepped-fret band (Mixtec greca, frieze group 2, bar style at 2px/design-unit): wave A stands ON a continuous 2-row `=` bar (10×10 head, 1px square-spiral groove entering from the left, 4-step staircase right), wave B is A's exact 180° rotation hanging upside-down below with its head under A's staircase; loops via `col % 20`, A/B colorways swap each period (defaults `#4b4b4b`/`#b3b3b3`) while the bar always renders the B color unswapped. Defaults to 3 periods (22×60, size 9, gap 0) so the mosaic repetition reads; cols snap by 20 / rows by 22 while on; mini-grid cells auto-shrink (14→10→8px past 32/48 cols); per-pixel overrides still win. The **tile board** is the page's default configuration (full-width card, on by default; the gradient builder + boxes/buttons/presets sit in a row below it, and only the heavy 3-layer pixel grid stays unmounted until the board is toggled off). Above everything, a **frieze header**: a thin full-bleed metallic strip at the very top (negative margins cancel the page padding so it touches the screen edges) — import two SVGs (or hit "use board waves" to pull the board's A/B masks directly), each becomes a `mask-image` on a stacked layer with its own palette-pickable color, `mask-repeat: repeat-x` repeats the frieze side-to-side, and the surface is a brushed-metal sheen gradient over a chooseable base color. Three geometry sliders: **height** 20-120 (thin by default, 44px) + top/bottom **padding** 0-24 + **frieze size** 15-100% — the motif's height as a % of the strip (`mask-size: auto <scale>%`), so shrinking it tiles MORE repetitions to complete the band. A **glass overlayer** (`.mercury-frieze-glass`, `glassStyle`) is a direct child of the header (not the padded inner), so it spans the FULL strip height — over the friezes AND the padded metal margins — a palette-pickable tint at a 0-0.85 **opacity** slider (`hexToRgba`) under a fixed diagonal white sheen gradient, with an optional **glass edge** toggle adding a tinted 1px border + bevelled inset highlight/shadow box-shadow. A **carve** control (`carve` = depth/light/shadow) applies stacked `drop-shadow` filters to each masked silhouette — a `dark` edge up-left + a `light` edge down-right, each doubled (full + half offset) so the coloured edge fills a solid band rather than a hairline (depth 0-5px) — so the two frieze patterns read as needle-cut into the glass-metallic plaque (defaults depth 1.5, light `#ffffff`, shadow `#0b0c10`); light/shadow are palette-pickable. The board itself is a single-layer two-color drawing strip: one editable tile (chooseable dims, 4-40×4-32, LED-style flat cells on dark) rendered 3× side by side from the same `row-col` map via `col % cols` — painting any repetition (wave A / wave B / erase tools + drag strokes, two neon color swatches) updates all three live, and strokes wrap across the faint seam guides, for testing horizontal tile continuity; while it's on the 3-layer pixel grid, boxes, buttons and presets cards are unmounted to save resources. Tiles export/import as plain text (`MERCURY-TILE v1` header, `cols/rows/size/a/b` settings, then `map:` + one line per row with `.`/`a`/`b`): the **save** button copies the text to the clipboard (mirrored into the textarea as fallback) for keeping in notes, and **interpret** parses pasted text back — resizing the board, restoring both colors and relighting the matrix (tolerant parser: trims blanks, clamps dims to slider ranges, case-insensitive); round-trips exactly. A radius slider (0-50% of cell size) rounds the board pixels and rides along in the text format (`radius:` line, optional on import); SVG downloads come **one pattern per wave**: **svg A** / **svg B** save `mercury-wave-a/b.svg` with only that wave's cells on a TRANSPARENT canvas filled PURE WHITE `#ffffff` (the neutral mask base — use as CSS `mask-image` and paint the element any color, or tint via blend modes/filters), **svg both** saves `mercury-tile.svg` with the dark `#0b0c10` bg baked in; all three share the identical cols×rows canvas so they tile seamlessly and layer in perfect registration. Board cells render with NO gap and SVGs draw gapless un-rounded rects under `shape-rendering="crispEdges"`, so exported drawings are continuous figures with no pixel seams (the radius slider now only rounds the on-screen cells). Self-contained page; palette hexes are local constants, not app SCSS vars |

The comments pane embeds `PostMakerSurface` as its composer (draft parented to the post; closing parks it as a dock tab). `?unravel=<id,…>` deep-links open the comments pane, expand the chain and halo the target comment (reloading once when the target is newer than the loaded list). Attachments and the `contentElements` machinery were removed 2026-07-14 (refs live inline in the markdown). The old `PostDetailPage.vue` was deleted — the router has used `PostViewerPage` since |
| `/skeletons` | `SkeletonPage` (list) | ⭐ Skeleton explorer — one dig-in square per **origin** skeleton (`skeletons/SkeletonSquares.vue`); the dug pit lists instantiations as identifying rows (`skeletons/SkeletonInstanceList.vue`: title/compacted name, author, age, excerpt, `SkeletonRefLinks`, per-row dig for instances-of-instances, "+ N more…" pagination). Selecting a square or row feeds the right-hand Structure panel (`skeletons/SkeletonUsages.vue`). |
| `/skeletons/:id` | `SkeletonPage` (detail) | ⭐ Spine-pairs navigator. Drives the **pathos-docs viewer** when the skeleton is `PATHOS_DOCS`. The header shows the skeleton's role as its real classification label chip (`SCHEMA` / `INSTANTIATION` in the labels band — the grey mode badges are gone; the name is the title: data, not a label). Schemas get **Instantiate** + **Fork** buttons (Fork opens the builder dock's fork draft via `skeletonBuilder.openForkDraft` — works on your own schemas too); non-POST instances get a **Fork** button (`POST /skeletons/:id/fork` → navigates to the fork). A **Forks band** under the subject panel lists direct forks (`GET /skeletons/:id/forks`) — on `/skeletons/5` (ELEMENT) that's the whole per-kind schema family. `ElementActions` hides its own fork entry here (`hide-fork`) so there's exactly one affordance. An **Export dropdown** (2026-08-01, both header branches) offers Bundle (3 layers deep w/ chain evidence) and Rows (instances as tabular data) — `skeletonService.exportDeep` → `GET /skeletons/:id/export` → JSON blob download named `pathos-<name>-<id>-<format>.json`; a 403 root stays with the AccessDeniedBanner path. **CLAIM instances lead with the claim band** (2026-07-31, `claims/ClaimBand.vue` — mounted in BOTH branches: above the read-only structure panel, and between header and editor for the owned author view).  **Both header branches wear `sym_o_orthopedics`** (2026-08-07, user ask) — the Material Symbols glyph, at 20px, replacing the owned-editor's hard-coded `schema` and the read-only branch's `iconForSkeleton(skeleton)`. That helper answers with the per-KIND icon (a POST reads `article`), which states what the instance is ABOUT where this line is the VIEWER's own title; it still dresses the usage and fork row lists below, where per-kind is exactly what tells the rows apart. The feed card's cap uses the same glyph for its way in here, so the door and the room wear one sign. |

| `/entities/:id` | `EntityProfilePage` | Entity viewer (subject-panel chrome + Anchors rail). On your own profile the Anchors rail adds a **wallet block** (DOG Coin C1, 2026-08-01: balance headline + the 5 latest movements with direction arrows and memo lines, each row's receipt an `InfoChip` into the payer-signed TRANSACTION skeleton; `wallet.service.js` → `GET /api/wallet`), the invite-secret generator (since 2026-08-01 it wears the **doubling-wait curve**: a status line off `GET /identity/invite-status` — "Invitation #11 · unlocks in 2m", inherited handicap called out — the button disabled while locked, and a 429 from generate surfaced as "Not yet — invitation #N unlocks in …"), a **"your secrets"** list (status chip `used`/`unused`; used rows link to the receiving entity via `GET /api/secrets/mine`; `reset`-type rows read `reset · <status>` with "recovers @user" — receiver bound at mint), a **sessions** block (Thread H, 2026-07-31: `GET /identity/sessions` → device rows with a glance label parsed from the user agent — "Chrome · Mac" — "this device" chip on the current row, revoke buttons on the rest, "last seen" times), and a big **Log out** button below the panel (`authStore.logout()` → best-effort `POST /identity/logout` server-side revoke, then clears localStorage → `/#/auth`). Viewing a person **you invited** (their `ancestor_id` = your root, type USER) shows a **"password recovery"** block in the Anchors rail: mint button → `POST /identity/recovery/mint` → the one-time reset secret hash to hand over out of band. When the viewed entity is in *your identity tree* (your login or any of its alter-egos), an **Alter-egos panel** (`entities/AlterEgoPanel.vue`) joins the rail: acting-as banner, "return to root identity" button, the entity's nested sub-entity tree (per-node internal log-in buttons via `authStore.switchIdentity`), and the alter-ego creation module (name + optional bio → `POST /api/entities/:id/alter-egos`). Below the grid, a full-width **contributions band** (`entities/EntityContributions.vue`): three panels — **Instantiations** (every owned template instance via `GET /entities/:id/skeletons`, schema link per row, "plumbing" toggle for ELEMENT/PATH/PIN/NAVIGATION), **Posts** (`GET /feed?ownerId=`, title + excerpt rows), **Content** (`GET /nodes/uploads?ownerId=`, thumbnail mini-gallery — images inline, other kinds as icon+ext tiles), each paginated with a load-more. Own profiles also get the **"My organizations" band** (`entities/EntityOrganizations.vue`, above My polls): each membership cell shows the org, role title, admin chip and the MASK with its put-on-the-mask switch — masks live in the ORG's folder, so this band (not the alter-ego tree) is where they surface. Org entities (type 3) hide the AlterEgoPanel — masks mint only through the join seam |
